**Package:** `finite_qm` in `omega_engine_atlas/coda_atlas/F004_papers/006_finite_qm`.

**AuthorityEffect:** None.

**Current projection:** 2026-10-21. The 834 source-claim rows are the projection of
2026-10-14 from [claims/claim_ledger.yaml](claims/claim_ledger.yaml). Three sections were
added by hand after that projection and are not in the YAML: `SRC-U1-BORN` (2026-10-20),
and `SRC-PR-RESULTS` and `SRC-PFP` (2026-10-21; the second is generated from
`dag/derivation_dag.json` by `papers/core/tools/build_results_audit_table.py`).
The historical application label `apps/finite_qm`, original generation date
2026-09-04 and citation `sealed-derivation-v1 (3100c4e)` describe the imported
ledger revision. They are not a current publication or proof certificate.

**Sources:** the 67 numbered source packages and the WDP source rows named in
the canonical YAML. Their historical observations and separate current-use
decisions are recorded in [source_inventory.yaml](claims/source_inventory.yaml).
The 13 changed originals and five divergent retained manuscripts are reconciled
for the pinned current imports under GAP-FQM-024/025. This does not replay a
historical proof receipt or equate unrecovered source versions.

## Read this first

Every row below copies the current statement, type, evidence and historical
source-status field from the canonical YAML. A historical source label such as
PROVEN is a quoted source observation; the current Type column and stated
premises control its use. The distribution counts classifications, not fresh
successful executions. Source restoration does not validate stale headers,
and no present-day tree-wide stub census is asserted here.

The manuscript distinguishes general written arguments, mechanized statements
with quantified premises, exact finite checks and numerical examples. Current
premises and remaining obligations are in its sections 1, 3, 11 and 13 and in
[gap_register.yaml](claims/gap_register.yaml).

## Distribution

The table counts every row of every claims table below, by its Type. The 834 rows of the
canonical projection of 2026-10-14 are joined by the 7 `FQM-U1-C*` rows, the rows of
`SRC-PR-RESULTS` and the 151 rows of `SRC-PFP`; the Total is the sum of all of them. The
counts classify rows. They are not fresh successful executions.

**Taxonomy change, 2026-10-21.** The seven `FQM-U1-C1`..`FQM-U1-C7` rows were typed
`PROVED_LEAN`, a type that the taxonomy did not contain, and the Total of 834 omitted them.
The taxonomy now contains `PROVED_LEAN`, defined in the table, and those rows keep their
type. The type is used for rows whose statement, at the scope written in the row, is stated by
named Lean 4 declarations. It is deliberately separate from `LEAN PROVED (RECEIPTED)`, which
quotes a historical receipted source classification. Rows keyed by a DAG node use the type
that matches the node's current status (`PROVED_LEAN`, `FINITE COMPUTATION` for
`COMPUTATIONALLY_CHECKED`, `CONDITIONAL`, `DECLARED` for `IMPORTED_ASSUMPTION` and
`ADMITTED_PRIMITIVE`, `DEFINITION`, `OPEN`), so a row whose review lowered the status carries
the lowered type.

## Current foundational scope

These programme summaries retain the old FQM identifiers for navigation. They
are separate from the claim rows counted in the Distribution. Full premises and
the distinction between algebra, Lean coverage and numerical examples are in
[manuscript section 1.1](finite_quantum_mechanics.md#11-the-8-rung-foundational-walk-rungs-1-to-8).

| Historical programme ID | Current scoped statement | Evidence boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| FQM-E1 | A density matrix is defined to be Hermitian, positive semidefinite and unit trace on a supplied matrix carrier. | Exact matrix predicates and numerical demonstrations have different implementation scopes; section 12 identifies the exact owner. |
| FQM-E2 | The declared depolarizing map scales a trace-zero difference by 1-p; it contracts trace distance at admitted CPTP parameters. | Section 11.35 gives the exact dimension/parameter checks and sampled pairs. |
| FQM-E3 | The supplied polar/sign witnesses attain the trace norm on the stated packets. | A quotient-duality certificate additionally requires the admissible repair space and correct dual constraint; section 11.35. |
| FQM-E4 | The declared Mermin-Peres grid has the specified commutation and parity products. | Fixed finite observable/context packet; sections 11.33 and 11.50. |
| FQM-E5 | The declared preparation, channel and projective-update examples preserve their checked state constraints. | Finite implementation checks do not derive preparations or measurement availability. |
| FQM-E6, FQM-F1 | On the declared integer subtraction carrier, D(x,y)=y-x is antisymmetric, satisfies the cocycle identity, and vanishes exactly when x=y. | Subtraction and equality on that carrier are supplied; this does not derive a native report relation or quantum carrier. |
| FQM-E7, FQM-F2 | Boundary maps of the declared finite two-complex satisfy d1 d0=0; stated circulation witnesses distinguish its exact and non-exact classes. | Selected Lean algebra is separate from the floating-point HiGHS quotient-mass implementation and its 1e-9 tolerance. |
| FQM-E8, FQM-E9 | Quotient mass is invariant under adding an admitted coboundary and supports the declared class diagnostic. | The legacy Python LP returns numerical values, not exact optimum/zero certificates; exact Rust packet certificates are separately scoped. |
| FQM-F3 | Positive-total nonnegative weights normalize to a scale-invariant profile. Born normalization follows from a supplied density/effect pairing and a POVM sum equal to identity. | The Born square, Hilbert carrier and effect domain remain imported; section 1.1 separates the general trace identity from the narrower Lean model. |
| FQM-F4 | Declared phase-sensitive mixing can distinguish equal diagonal profiles, and a quadratic readout has vanishing third-order interference. | Zero holonomy can still have a nonzero coherent cross term; the square and continuous phase carrier are not selected by this cancellation. |
| FQM-F5 | A supplied tensor carrier admits non-product vectors and the stated finite contextuality packet. | Neither the finite witnesses nor exclusion of one rival establishes uniqueness of composition. |
| FQM-F6 | Declared unitary steps preserve the supplied norm. Discrete dynamics do not select a continuous interpolation or Hamiltonian logarithm branch. | The QM_034/QM_035 entries in section 11 give the field- and carrier-dependent half-step and generator tests. |
| FQM-F7 | Supplied positive effects summing to identity give normalized nonnegative Born probabilities; declared channels and updates obey their stated quantum definitions. | A selected Kraus family may require square roots, while a larger rational family can avoid that extension; sections 1.1 and 12. |
| FQM-F8 | The finite constructions retain explicit boundaries at carrier selection, Born representation, composition, continuum interfaces and individual outcome selection. | No unconditional reconstruction is claimed; sections 5 and 13. |
| FQM-B1, FQM-B2 | A positive global four-response law gives the exact CHSH bound $|S|\leq2$; a supplied rational tensor model reaches $S=14/5>2$ and has no positive global joint extension. | Exact finite algebra under the stated joint-law and quantum-model premises; sections 11.33 and 13.8.2. |
| FQM-B3, FQM-B4 | Exact sum-of-squares certificates give $|S|\leq2\sqrt2$, and a static real $\mathbb Q(\sqrt2)$ witness attains equality. | The witness shows that specifically complex phase and continuous dynamics are not part of the static Bell minimum. |
| FQM-B5 | The Bell endpoint uses supplied composition, entangled state, observables, positivity and trace/Born pairing. | Scope fence: these inputs are not derived from primitive difference; section 13.8.2. |
| FQM-B6, FQM-B7 | Compatible context distributions express no-signalling/no-disturbance on the declared cover; a support-derived nonzero Čech class is a sufficient, incomplete contextuality witness. The scalar $\max(0,|S|-2)$ is only a CHSH violation margin. | A selected obstruction class is not the whole $H^1$ group, and its construction requires a coefficient presheaf and local section; section 13.8.3. |
| FQM-B8 | The Mermin--Peres target is outside the image of the exact $\mathbb F_2$ observables-to-contexts incidence map. | Its cohomological degree depends on a separately declared complex, while its quantum reading uses supplied Pauli operators; sections 11.50 and 13.8.3. |
| FQM-B9 | Regge deficit angles diagnose simplicial curvature but do not presently determine contextuality or a Bell bound. | RQ-10 returns `Unknown`; no `gamma_Cech = |epsilon|` or `S_CHSH <= min(4, 2 + |epsilon|)` theorem is claimed; section 13.8.3. |
| FQM-DAG1 | The derivation DAG `dag/derivation_dag.json` is a map of records from the inert inscription `<>` to finite quantum mechanics. It has 737 nodes and 734 routes (205 `PROVED_LEAN`, 148 `OPEN`, 143 `COMPUTATIONALLY_CHECKED`, 81 `DEFINITION`, 55 `SOURCE_RECORD`, 45 `IMPORTED_ASSUMPTION`, 28 `CONDITIONAL`, 27 `PROVED_PROSE`, 3 `ADMITTED_PRIMITIVE`, 2 `REFUTED`; 157 routes are `GAP_NO_DERIVATION`). It is joined to the 789 records of `dag/theory_of_finite_qm.jsonl`. The Lean module `lean4/DerivationDagQMClosure.lean` states finite fixtures and generic algebraic identities for a subset of nodes. | A node's status labels that node's statement only. The DAG is not a mechanization of the programme: only 191 of 737 nodes cite two or more Lean declarations and two or more Rust witnesses. 151 nodes carry a PFP review that narrowed 52 statements and lowered 22 statuses (section 13.8.12 and the SRC-PFP rows below). The earlier statement for a 177-node, 172-route DAG (125 `PROVED_LEAN`, 7 `CONDITIONAL`, 25 `IMPORTED_ASSUMPTION`, 14 `DEFINITION`, 3 `ADMITTED_PRIMITIVE`, 3 `OPEN`; 112 Lean theorems; 30 Rust witness families) is retired: it was a snapshot, the Rust witness file is not in this checkout, and the counts were not re-verified. Continuum, infinite-dimensional and imported Hilbert/Born/tensor/CPTP assumptions remain explicitly separated; section 13.8.8. Counts regenerate with `papers/core/tools/build_results_audit_table.py`. |

Campaign-specific FQM-C identifiers are not part of the canonical 834-row source
ledger. Their dated campaign artifacts retain their own revision meaning;
current admitted consequences must be read with the manuscript's explicit
premises and current gap dispositions. This projection does not carry forward
an unqualified campaign-wide proof or closure label.

## Historical review summary -- 2026-10-12

This dated summary retains the scope of that review, not a new proof replay.

These entries supplement the historical ledger; archived claims and source pins
retain their original revision meaning. Full premises and reproduction records:

| Current claim | Evidence class | Exact limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Normalized clock seed yields a verified Weyl intertwiner | Exact Rust construction; d=3 and d=6 regressions | Supplied primitive root, unitary Weyl pair, period and seed; no general irrep classification |
| Distinct clock plus shift-entry propagation forces a scalar commutant | Lean theorem over a field in every positive finite dimension | The clock spectrum and entrywise shift premise are inputs |
| Binary rational-plane frame function is nonquadratic | Lean theorem | All nonzero rational two-vectors and rational rescalings; no d>=3 uniqueness claim |
| Cabello nine-context integer assignment is impossible | Lean parity theorem | Fixed 18-ray configuration; not uniqueness of probability-valued frames |
| Four qubit effect readouts determine/recover a Hermitian candidate | Lean theorem | Global positive representation of the frame function is still open |
| Finite nonnegative frame data can reconstruct a non-PSD matrix | Lean witness and exact Rust regression | Exact density-state admission adds an independent PSD check |
| 864 declared noncyclic candidates are excluded against the fixed Weyl triad | Exact finite Rust enumeration | No row-phase or continuous-family exhaustion; no unrestricted MUB6 upper bound |

The seven rows are typed `PROVED_LEAN`; that type is in the taxonomy since 2026-10-21 (see the Distribution). Each row is limited to the scope written in it. The parent DAG nodes `FQ-GAP-U1-CIRCLE`, `FQ-GAP-BORN-UNIQUENESS-GLEASON` and `FQ-GAP-STONE-CONTINUOUS-TIME` are `CONDITIONAL`, and the compact circle, Born uniqueness and Stone's theorem are not proved by these rows.

### SRC-PFP -- PFP result reviews, keyed by DAG node

Generated on 2026-10-21 from the `pfp_review` object of each reviewed node of `dag/derivation_dag.json` by `papers/core/tools/build_results_audit_table.py`; do not edit by hand. *Claim* is the review's narrowest claim, which is all the row asserts. The corresponding non-claim (`does_not_support`) is in the table of manuscript section 13.8.12. *Type* follows the node's current status (`COMPUTATIONALLY_CHECKED` is typed `FINITE COMPUTATION`; `IMPORTED_ASSUMPTION` and `ADMITTED_PRIMITIVE` are typed `DECLARED`). *Evidence* is the review file; *DAG status* gives the node status and the scope_effect, with the earlier status where the review lowered it.
